# Step 15's analyzer-round RESULTS entry and Code's step-15 ping to the seat -- values-check before commit: two
# adversarial passes on the entry, two on the ping, then a targeted third check of both, then a mechanical quote check.
# Each full pass: two verifiers (one per half), a strict refuter on the flagged, a values lens. Read-only agents; no
# model; data/m/ckpt never opened; the G-F0m-Qwen stage raw and the Q-F0 report not opened.

####################################################################################################
ENTRY, PASS 1
####################################################################################################

======================================== THE SCRIPT
export const meta = {
  name: 'step15-entry-valuescheck',
  description: 'Values-check the step-15 analyzer-round RESULTS entry: two claim verifiers, strict refuter, values lens',
  phases: [
    { title: 'Verify', detail: 'every atomic claim, two halves' },
    { title: 'Refute', detail: 'strict refuter on flagged claims' },
    { title: 'Values', detail: 'values, blinding and completeness lens' },
  ],
}
const REPO = '/Users/jasondwyer/Documents/code/jspace-lab'
const SCR = '/private/tmp/claude-501/-Users-jasondwyer-Documents-code-jspace-lab/adb70afc-5886-490b-a675-abb30f8c425f/scratchpad/step15'
const PASS = args && args.pass ? args.pass : 1
const COMMON = `
CONTEXT. Repository ${REPO}. Code has written a new top entry in RESULTS.md (uncommitted; git diff RESULTS.md): "2026-10-14 — THE R2 3 ANALYZER ROUND AND THE G-ANL-F0 RE-RUN ..." (the first entry under "## Log — Phase 2", running to the line "...valuescheck.txt))." before the "2026-10-13 — G-F0m-Qwen" entry). This is values-check pass ${PASS} before commit. Sources: the commit b046b16 (git show --stat b046b16; its message); scripts/m_analyze.py (committed, sha256 must be 522e8057...fd3c); results/raw/2026-10-14_m-analyzer-round-step15_review.txt (committed at b046b16; the review raw, verbatim records); results/raw/2026-10-14_m-ganl-f0-rerun-step15.txt and results/raw/2026-10-14_m-analyzer-round-step15_sitecheck.txt (uncommitted, to be committed with the entry); data/m/gates/G-ANL-F0.json (new, uncommitted) and data/m/gates/G-ANL-F0.superseded-2026-10-14.json (the old manifest, moved; compare them with python3 json); LOCK-m-F0.md (v0.1 text §9.3, §9.6; v0.2 §§0, 8); the seat's rulings results/raw/2026-10-10_m-design-to-code_pre-LOCK-v0.1.md (R1) and ..._round2.md (R2); the step-14 read results/raw/2026-10-13_m-design-to-code_G-F0m-Qwen_read.md; the step-10 read results/raw/2026-10-13_m-design-to-code_G-TIME-F0_read.md; PROPOSAL-m-gating-campaign-v1_11.md (m:N = line N); valence_core/m/gshape.py; scratch records under ${SCR} (final_r6_summary.txt, extra_r6_summary.txt, sweep_r6_summary.txt, equivalents.json, sess/, sess_lock/). The PI's routing of 2026-10-13 is quoted in the review raw.
HARD RULES: read-only; no edits; no git command that writes; never run git inside ${REPO}/jlens-qwen36; no model; NEVER open data/m/ckpt/*, results/raw/2026-10-13_m-gf0m-qwen.txt or data/m/reports/Q-F0_qual_report.json; compute no contrast timing.`
const CLAIMS = { type: 'object', properties: { claims: { type: 'array', items: { type: 'object', properties: {
  id: { type: 'string' }, text: { type: 'string' }, verdict: { type: 'string', enum: ['VERIFIED', 'FAILS', 'IMPRECISE', 'UNCHECKABLE'] },
  evidence: { type: 'string' }, correction: { type: 'string' } }, required: ['id', 'text', 'verdict', 'evidence', 'correction'] } } }, required: ['claims'] }
const REF = { type: 'object', properties: { verdicts: { type: 'array', items: { type: 'object', properties: {
  id: { type: 'string' }, verdict: { type: 'string', enum: ['CONFIRMED', 'REFUTED', 'PLAUSIBLE'] }, evidence: { type: 'string' }, correction: { type: 'string' } },
  required: ['id', 'verdict', 'evidence', 'correction'] } } }, required: ['verdicts'] }
const VALUES = { type: 'object', properties: { findings: { type: 'array', items: { type: 'object', properties: {
  id: { type: 'string' }, severity: { type: 'string', enum: ['major', 'minor', 'note'] }, finding: { type: 'string' }, suggested_text: { type: 'string' } },
  required: ['id', 'severity', 'finding', 'suggested_text'] } } }, required: ['findings'] }
const VT = (half, p) => `${COMMON}\nTASK: extract EVERY atomic claim in ${half} of the entry (every hash, count, path, commit, quote, PIN, line cite, attribution, number) and check each with tools. Quoted passages must be verbatim from their source. Inclusive claims ("every", "all", "none", "no other", "nothing") need an inventory: check them. Number claims ${p}01, ${p}02, ... Verdicts VERIFIED / FAILS / IMPRECISE / UNCHECKABLE with evidence and exact minimal corrections.`
phase('Verify')
const vs = await parallel([
  ['the FIRST HALF (from the entry heading through the end of the bullet "**The edit**" and its sub-bullets, ending at "...(`package_sha256` `5f646db6…d771`).")', 'A'],
  ['the SECOND HALF (from the bullet "**Review**" through the end of the entry)', 'B'],
].map(([h, p]) => () => agent(VT(h, p), { label: `verify-${p}`, phase: 'Verify', schema: CLAIMS })))
const claims = vs.filter(Boolean).flatMap(x => x.claims || [])
const flagged = claims.filter(c => c.verdict === 'FAILS' || c.verdict === 'IMPRECISE')
log(`pass ${PASS}: ${claims.length} claims, ${flagged.length} flagged`)
let r = null
if (flagged.length) {
  r = await agent(`${COMMON}\nTASK (STRICT refuter): for each flagged claim, re-check independently and try to show the entry's text is right. CONFIRMED = it must change (minimal exact correction); REFUTED = right as written; PLAUSIBLE = unsettled. Do not confirm style preferences.\nFLAGGED:\n${JSON.stringify(flagged, null, 1)}`, { label: 'refute', phase: 'Refute', schema: REF })
}
phase('Values')
const vals = await agent(`${COMMON}\nTASK (values lens${PASS > 1 ? '; pass ' + PASS + ', after pass 1 was applied: look for what pass 1 missed and what the rewrite broke' : ''}): read the entry whole as the seat and the PI will. Check: (1) does it record what R2 3, PIN-37, PIN-39, C8 and D3 require of an analyzer round and its re-run (compare the precedent entry "THE D2 + C9(ii) EDIT AND THE G-ANL-F0 RE-RUN" of 2026-10-10)? (2) any overclaim, especially about the review's and the mutation batch's coverage, the window, the site check; any omission that matters (set-aside or discarded runs, C8; what the seat must decide; what blocks v1.0); (3) attribution: the seat's words vs Code's readings; anything that reads as Code adjudicating or pre-deciding an item the seat or the PI owns (the preprint; the new readings' terms; R3M-5); (4) order and process: does anything here depart from the PI's word's order or R1 A2/A4, or from the step-14 read's Q4 rule; is the window effect stated as the record's own consequence (lock §9.3) and not as a defect; (5) blinding: nothing pick-bearing. Suggested replacement text for each finding.`, { label: 'values', phase: 'Values', schema: VALUES })
return { pass: PASS, claims, refuted: r ? r.verdicts : [], values: vals ? vals.findings : [] }

======================================== CODE'S DISPOSITIONS
Pass 1. The two verifiers checked 130 claims: 107 VERIFIED, 17 IMPRECISE, 3 FAILS, 3 UNCHECKABLE. The strict refuter
CONFIRMED 14 of the 20 flags and REFUTED 6 (A20, A27, A35, B01, B17, B39). The values
lens returned 18 findings (3 major). Every confirmed flag and every values finding was applied by rewriting the entry.
Three refuted flags were applied as well, because the change costs nothing: A20's "a lock revision (v1.0, ...)", A35
(V5), and B39 (V17's "No other key differs").
- V1 / B25 / B28 / B46 (major): the window case is now attributed correctly. The licence is stubbed in that case, so
  the message is the driver's own proposal-in-lock check (stage_identity_extra, m_sessions.py:541-545), not check (f).
  - Which licence check refuses (d or f) depends on the analyzer sha256 the command line gives; the entry now says so.
  - The site-check script's label had the same error. It was corrected and the site check re-run (11:11 UTC); the raw
    is the second run, and its header says so.
  - The committed review raw (b046b16) and the commit message carry the error. The entry corrects them.
- V2 / B11 / B18 (major): the batch is counted as 258 distinct mutants (by the mutated file's sha256) in 297 runs.
  - Code recomputed this: 297 runs, 258 distinct, no conflicting outcomes. Over distinct mutants: 239 killed, 12
    equivalent, 1 strengthening, 6 accepted gaps.
  - The by-run counts are kept beside. The entry corrects the review raw's and the commit message's counts.
- V3 (major): the table's place in the round is now stated as Code's reading, put to the seat in the ping (new Q9) as
  a departure under R1 A2 if the seat reads the order otherwise. It changes nothing the re-run took (the placeholder).
- V4 / A16 / A36: the copy is described one way throughout, as the close line reads §4 item 3.
- V5 (with A35): "needed" is now Code's reading, with its ground and the cost if wrong.
- V6: the closure's truncation is Code's reading, put to the seat.
- V7: Code's readings beyond R2 3's words are listed, including (c)'s widening beyond Code's round-2 block.
- V8 / A55 / A56: (b)'s pause rules and the class check are scoped.
- V9: "For the seat" gives each item's default, the cost and timing of a strike, PIN-45's limits, L2-9's effect, and
  what v1.0's commit waits on.
- V10: the battery's extension, by id family (Code recounted: 62 new ids = 33 + 10 + 18 + 1).
- V11: PIN-9's and PIN-10's stale "flagged for the seat" clauses, with Code's reading of R2 2's allowance; put to the
  seat (ping Q8).
- V12 / B32: G-PWR in the window, as Code's reading.
- V13: a "Committed with this entry" list; the unstaged git mv; the ping's path; the re-run's stamps; R2 3 as PIN-37's
  ruling; the stamp line records pass 1.
- V14: the round's two set-aside runs.
- V15: the read-only planning map before the close.
- V16: D3 at v1.0.
- V17 / B39: "No other key differs."
- B07: 107 sweep items = 106 citations + 1 scope note.
- B21: "probes or re-runs".
- B23: the lock §9.3 quote without "then".
- B30: the control is "the prior analyzer (66fb5214...7d5e, on a clone at 5af9e07)".
- V18: the blinding check passed; no change.
The ping (scratch ping15.md) was brought into line: the window text; Q8 (PIN-9's and PIN-10's clauses); Q9 (the
table's place); (c)'s widening in Q1; the cost and timing of answers.

======================================== THE SCRIPT
export const meta = {
  name: 'step15-entry-valuescheck',
  description: 'Values-check the step-15 analyzer-round RESULTS entry: two claim verifiers, strict refuter, values lens',
  phases: [
    { title: 'Verify', detail: 'every atomic claim, two halves' },
    { title: 'Refute', detail: 'strict refuter on flagged claims' },
    { title: 'Values', detail: 'values, blinding and completeness lens' },
  ],
}
const REPO = '/Users/jasondwyer/Documents/code/jspace-lab'
const SCR = '/private/tmp/claude-501/-Users-jasondwyer-Documents-code-jspace-lab/adb70afc-5886-490b-a675-abb30f8c425f/scratchpad/step15'
const PASS = args && args.pass ? args.pass : 1
const COMMON = `
CONTEXT. Repository ${REPO}. Code has written a new top entry in RESULTS.md (uncommitted; git diff RESULTS.md): "2026-10-14 — THE R2 3 ANALYZER ROUND AND THE G-ANL-F0 RE-RUN ..." (the first entry under "## Log — Phase 2", running to the line "    - The analyzer and the review raw are in \`b046b16\`." before the "2026-10-13 — G-F0m-Qwen" entry; pass 1's findings were applied by rewriting it). Code's step-15 ping, which the entry links as results/raw/2026-10-14_m-code-to-design_step15.md, is still in scratch at ${SCR}/ping15.md (to be committed with the entry); check the entry's claims about it against that file. The site-check raw is its second run (11:11 UTC); the first run is kept at ${SCR}/sitecheck_run1.txt. This is values-check pass ${PASS} before commit. Sources: the commit b046b16 (git show --stat b046b16; its message); scripts/m_analyze.py (committed, sha256 must be 522e8057...fd3c); results/raw/2026-10-14_m-analyzer-round-step15_review.txt (committed at b046b16; the review raw, verbatim records); results/raw/2026-10-14_m-ganl-f0-rerun-step15.txt and results/raw/2026-10-14_m-analyzer-round-step15_sitecheck.txt (uncommitted, to be committed with the entry); data/m/gates/G-ANL-F0.json (new, uncommitted) and data/m/gates/G-ANL-F0.superseded-2026-10-14.json (the old manifest, moved; compare them with python3 json); LOCK-m-F0.md (v0.1 text §9.3, §9.6; v0.2 §§0, 8); the seat's rulings results/raw/2026-10-10_m-design-to-code_pre-LOCK-v0.1.md (R1) and ..._round2.md (R2); the step-14 read results/raw/2026-10-13_m-design-to-code_G-F0m-Qwen_read.md; the step-10 read results/raw/2026-10-13_m-design-to-code_G-TIME-F0_read.md; PROPOSAL-m-gating-campaign-v1_11.md (m:N = line N); valence_core/m/gshape.py; scratch records under ${SCR} (final_r6_summary.txt, extra_r6_summary.txt, sweep_r6_summary.txt, equivalents.json, sess/, sess_lock/). The PI's routing of 2026-10-13 is quoted in the review raw.
HARD RULES: read-only; no edits; no git command that writes; never run git inside ${REPO}/jlens-qwen36; no model; NEVER open data/m/ckpt/*, results/raw/2026-10-13_m-gf0m-qwen.txt or data/m/reports/Q-F0_qual_report.json; compute no contrast timing.`
const CLAIMS = { type: 'object', properties: { claims: { type: 'array', items: { type: 'object', properties: {
  id: { type: 'string' }, text: { type: 'string' }, verdict: { type: 'string', enum: ['VERIFIED', 'FAILS', 'IMPRECISE', 'UNCHECKABLE'] },
  evidence: { type: 'string' }, correction: { type: 'string' } }, required: ['id', 'text', 'verdict', 'evidence', 'correction'] } } }, required: ['claims'] }
const REF = { type: 'object', properties: { verdicts: { type: 'array', items: { type: 'object', properties: {
  id: { type: 'string' }, verdict: { type: 'string', enum: ['CONFIRMED', 'REFUTED', 'PLAUSIBLE'] }, evidence: { type: 'string' }, correction: { type: 'string' } },
  required: ['id', 'verdict', 'evidence', 'correction'] } } }, required: ['verdicts'] }
const VALUES = { type: 'object', properties: { findings: { type: 'array', items: { type: 'object', properties: {
  id: { type: 'string' }, severity: { type: 'string', enum: ['major', 'minor', 'note'] }, finding: { type: 'string' }, suggested_text: { type: 'string' } },
  required: ['id', 'severity', 'finding', 'suggested_text'] } } }, required: ['findings'] }
const VT = (half, p) => `${COMMON}\nTASK: extract EVERY atomic claim in ${half} of the entry (every hash, count, path, commit, quote, PIN, line cite, attribution, number) and check each with tools. Quoted passages must be verbatim from their source. Inclusive claims ("every", "all", "none", "no other", "nothing") need an inventory: check them. Number claims ${p}01, ${p}02, ... Verdicts VERIFIED / FAILS / IMPRECISE / UNCHECKABLE with evidence and exact minimal corrections.`
phase('Verify')
const vs = await parallel([
  ['the FIRST HALF (from the entry heading through the end of the bullet "**The edit**" and its sub-bullets, ending at "...this round, before v1.0, is the cheap point (the ping).")', 'A'],
  ['the SECOND HALF (from the bullet "**Review**" through the end of the entry)', 'B'],
].map(([h, p]) => () => agent(VT(h, p), { label: `verify-${p}`, phase: 'Verify', schema: CLAIMS })))
const claims = vs.filter(Boolean).flatMap(x => x.claims || [])
const flagged = claims.filter(c => c.verdict === 'FAILS' || c.verdict === 'IMPRECISE')
log(`pass ${PASS}: ${claims.length} claims, ${flagged.length} flagged`)
let r = null
if (flagged.length) {
  r = await agent(`${COMMON}\nTASK (STRICT refuter): for each flagged claim, re-check independently and try to show the entry's text is right. CONFIRMED = it must change (minimal exact correction); REFUTED = right as written; PLAUSIBLE = unsettled. Do not confirm style preferences.\nFLAGGED:\n${JSON.stringify(flagged, null, 1)}`, { label: 'refute', phase: 'Refute', schema: REF })
}
phase('Values')
const vals = await agent(`${COMMON}\nTASK (values lens${PASS > 1 ? '; pass ' + PASS + ', after pass 1 was applied: look for what pass 1 missed and what the rewrite broke' : ''}): read the entry whole as the seat and the PI will. Check: (1) does it record what R2 3, PIN-37, PIN-39, C8 and D3 require of an analyzer round and its re-run (compare the precedent entry "THE D2 + C9(ii) EDIT AND THE G-ANL-F0 RE-RUN" of 2026-10-10)? (2) any overclaim, especially about the review's and the mutation batch's coverage, the window, the site check; any omission that matters (set-aside or discarded runs, C8; what the seat must decide; what blocks v1.0); (3) attribution: the seat's words vs Code's readings; anything that reads as Code adjudicating or pre-deciding an item the seat or the PI owns (the preprint; the new readings' terms; R3M-5); (4) order and process: does anything here depart from the PI's word's order or R1 A2/A4, or from the step-14 read's Q4 rule; is the window effect stated as the record's own consequence (lock §9.3) and not as a defect; (5) blinding: nothing pick-bearing. Suggested replacement text for each finding.`, { label: 'values', phase: 'Values', schema: VALUES })
return { pass: PASS, claims, refuted: r ? r.verdicts : [], values: vals ? vals.findings : [] }

======================================== CODE'S DISPOSITIONS
Pass 2. The two verifiers checked 139 claims: 122 VERIFIED, 11 IMPRECISE, 4 FAILS, 2 UNCHECKABLE. Of the flags, 10 were
CONFIRMED or PLAUSIBLE and A72 and B28 were REFUTED. The values lens returned 15 findings (4 major). Code applied every
confirmed and plausible flag and every values finding.
- VC2-01 / B29 / B31 (major): "Corrections to b046b16's records" is now its own bullet. The commit message names no
  check, so its window text stands. The run-versus-mutant count is exact: 39 repeat runs, 38 of them among the killed,
  plus a-4. Two more corrections are added: "107 citations", and "33/38" holding by id only.
- VC2-02 / B42 / B47 / B52 (major): PIN-45's limits are in the ping, with a default. "Each of the ping's items" now
  states what Code does without an answer. "What v1.0's commit follows" (VC2-08) now includes G-PWR, D3 and D1's
  source.
- VC2-03 (major): the claim that the table order "keeps the window open" is withdrawn. A copy placed after step 15
  began is not Code's to settle. The table now waits for the PI's word on the copy, which can come in the ping's routing
  (the ping's pass-2 V2-PI1-window, adopted in both documents).
- VC2-04 (major): the PIN index. Code compared every marker block in 5af9e07's and b046b16's analyzer:
  - P311 (the old STR label) is gone, and v0.1 markers go from 48 to 47;
  - P313 is extended;
  - three v1.0 sites are added;
  - the other 46 blocks are byte-identical.
  This is disclosed in the entry and put to the seat in ping Q1.
- VC2-05 / A52: the readings' source is the build spec plus the round-3 and round-4 reports.
- VC2-09 / VC2-10: the window's source is R2 3's sequence; the window opens at b046b16.
- VC2-11: (c)'s widening is treated as a possible departure in the same way as the table's place, with Code's ground:
  the block's heading names no STOP.
- VC2-12 / B41: the site check's and the re-run's stamps are given as start and finish times. Run 1 is kept in
  scratch. The basis for "working tree = HEAD" is stated.
- VC2-13: the read-only planning map is Code's reading under R1 A4, disclosed.
- VC2-14: the blinding procedure is stated in Review.
- VC2-15: D3's "states that diff" applies to the analyzer by Code's analogy. The superseded manifest's hash is noted.
- A37: "one analyzer round". A39: m:286 alone. A70: the positive control is named. A78: PIN-10's own wording. B24 and
  B53: m_sessions.py:542-545.
- After the ping's pass 2, the closure moved from a question to a settled note in the ping. The entry's closure item
  now says so.

======================================== THE SCRIPT
export const meta = {
  name: 'step15-ping-valuescheck',
  description: 'Values-check Code step-15 ping to the seat: two claim verifiers, strict refuter, values lens',
  phases: [
    { title: 'Verify', detail: 'every atomic claim, two halves' },
    { title: 'Refute', detail: 'strict refuter on flagged claims' },
    { title: 'Values', detail: 'values lens' },
  ],
}
const REPO = '/Users/jasondwyer/Documents/code/jspace-lab'
const SCR = '/private/tmp/claude-501/-Users-jasondwyer-Documents-code-jspace-lab/adb70afc-5886-490b-a675-abb30f8c425f/scratchpad/step15'
const PASS = args && args.pass ? args.pass : 1
const COMMON = `
CONTEXT. Repository ${REPO}. Code's step-15 ping to the design seat, for the PI to route, is drafted at ${SCR}/ping15.md (to be committed as results/raw/2026-10-14_m-code-to-design_step15.md beside the RESULTS entry "2026-10-14 — THE R2 3 ANALYZER ROUND AND THE G-ANL-F0 RE-RUN", uncommitted at the top of RESULTS.md). This is values-check pass ${PASS} before commit. Sources: the analyzer commit b046b16 (scripts/m_analyze.py, sha256 522e8057...fd3c; the review raw results/raw/2026-10-14_m-analyzer-round-step15_review.txt); the new manifest data/m/gates/G-ANL-F0.json and the moved one data/m/gates/G-ANL-F0.superseded-2026-10-14.json; LOCK-m-F0.md (v0.2 at top; the v0.1 text from "# The v0.1 text", its §§1, 9.1, 9.3, 9.6, 10, 11); PROPOSAL-m-gating-campaign-v1_11.md (m:N = line N); PREPRINT-v1_0-draft.md (the root copy; git ls-tree note-2026-10-v1); the seat's rulings results/raw/2026-10-10_m-design-to-code_pre-LOCK-v0.1.md (R1) and ..._round2.md (R2); the step-10 read results/raw/2026-10-13_m-design-to-code_G-TIME-F0_read.md; the step-14 read results/raw/2026-10-13_m-design-to-code_G-F0m-Qwen_read.md; RESULTS.md and git show 6920b4b:RESULTS.md; valence_core/m/bars.py, stages.py, schedule.py; scripts/m_sessions.py; DESIGN-to-CODE-PROPOSAL-m-v1.2-decisions.md (untracked); the review rounds under ${SCR} (r1_reviews.json ... r3_reviews.json, build/report_r3.md).
HARD RULES: read-only; no edits; no git command that writes; never run git inside ${REPO}/jlens-qwen36; no model; NEVER open data/m/ckpt/*, results/raw/2026-10-13_m-gf0m-qwen.txt or data/m/reports/Q-F0_qual_report.json; compute no contrast timing.`
const CLAIMS = { type: 'object', properties: { claims: { type: 'array', items: { type: 'object', properties: {
  id: { type: 'string' }, text: { type: 'string' }, verdict: { type: 'string', enum: ['VERIFIED', 'FAILS', 'IMPRECISE', 'UNCHECKABLE'] },
  evidence: { type: 'string' }, correction: { type: 'string' } }, required: ['id', 'text', 'verdict', 'evidence', 'correction'] } } }, required: ['claims'] }
const REF = { type: 'object', properties: { verdicts: { type: 'array', items: { type: 'object', properties: {
  id: { type: 'string' }, verdict: { type: 'string', enum: ['CONFIRMED', 'REFUTED', 'PLAUSIBLE'] }, evidence: { type: 'string' }, correction: { type: 'string' } },
  required: ['id', 'verdict', 'evidence', 'correction'] } } }, required: ['verdicts'] }
const VALUES = { type: 'object', properties: { findings: { type: 'array', items: { type: 'object', properties: {
  id: { type: 'string' }, severity: { type: 'string', enum: ['major', 'minor', 'note'] }, finding: { type: 'string' }, suggested_text: { type: 'string' } },
  required: ['id', 'severity', 'finding', 'suggested_text'] } } }, required: ['findings'] }
const VT = (half, p) => `${COMMON}\nTASK: extract EVERY atomic claim in ${half} of the ping (every quotation, line cite, hash, count, attribution, default, cost) and check each with tools. Quotations must be verbatim from their source (say where any differs). A paraphrase of the seat or the PI must not change meaning or scope. Number claims ${p}01, ... Verdicts VERIFIED / FAILS / IMPRECISE / UNCHECKABLE with evidence and exact minimal corrections.`
phase('Verify')
const vs = await parallel([
  ['the FIRST HALF (from the title through the end of Q3)', 'A'],
  ['the SECOND HALF (from Q4 through the end, "Not questions" included)', 'B'],
].map(([h, p]) => () => agent(VT(h, p), { label: `verify-${p}`, phase: 'Verify', schema: CLAIMS })))
const claims = vs.filter(Boolean).flatMap(x => x.claims || [])
const flagged = claims.filter(c => c.verdict === 'FAILS' || c.verdict === 'IMPRECISE')
log(`pass ${PASS}: ${claims.length} claims, ${flagged.length} flagged`)
let r = null
if (flagged.length) {
  r = await agent(`${COMMON}\nTASK (STRICT refuter): for each flagged claim, re-check independently and try to show the ping's text is right. CONFIRMED = it must change (minimal exact correction); REFUTED = right as written; PLAUSIBLE = unsettled.\nFLAGGED:\n${JSON.stringify(flagged, null, 1)}`, { label: 'refute', phase: 'Refute', schema: REF })
}
phase('Values')
const vals = await agent(`${COMMON}\nTASK (values lens${PASS > 1 ? '; pass ' + PASS + ', after pass 1 was applied' : ''}): read the ping whole as the seat and the PI will. Is each question necessary, clearly put, with its governing words exact, Code's default and the cost of each answer stated, and nothing pre-decided that is the seat's or the PI's? Is any question missing that the step-15 record raises (PIN-17/§8, the preprint, m:58 re-anchoring, the decisions block, G-PWR methods, truncation, the new readings, the table's place, R3M-5, PIN-9/PIN-10)? Is anything presented as a question that the record already settles (then it should be a note), or as a note that needs the seat's answer? Is the item for the PI separated from the seat's? Any overclaim (e.g. about what the battery or the mutation batch shows), or attribution error? Blinding: nothing pick-bearing. Suggested replacement text for each finding.`, { label: 'values', phase: 'Values', schema: VALUES })
return { pass: PASS, claims, refuted: r ? r.verdicts : [], values: vals ? vals.findings : [] }

======================================== CODE'S DISPOSITIONS
Pass 1. The two verifiers checked 123 claims: 98 VERIFIED, 18 IMPRECISE, 4 FAILS, 3 UNCHECKABLE. The strict refuter
CONFIRMED 15 of the 22 flags and REFUTED the rest (A07, A24, B62 among them). The values lens returned 14 findings
(4 major). Code rewrote the ping as a whole and applied every confirmed flag and every values finding. Three refuted
flags were applied too, since each costs nothing: A07 (V1-read-list), A24 (the v1.8 routing) and B62.
Changes by finding:
- V1-Q4-STOP (major): Q4 now sets out readings (A) and (B) evenly, with (B)'s cost (v1.0 waits; Q-F0 NOT-RUN past
  2026-10-30). It says the re-anchoring rests on PROPOSAL-m's descriptions of v1.1. The short form's "none stops"
  carries Q4(B) as its exception. B09 is applied there.
- V1-Q2-R3M5-residual (major): Q2 is named R3M-5. It states the stop→terminal limit that stays open either way, and the
  reviewers' two further candidates. A44's heading is fixed.
- V1-PIN45-limits-missing (major): PIN-45's three stated limits are added under "Not questions".
- V1-PI-item-question-window-deadline (major): PI 1 states the question, the window, the deadline (m:356 against
  m:286), the root copy's lacks, and the v1.8 routing (A24). A28: "costs no rebuild".
- V1-PI-other-items: PI 2 (v1.1's text), PI 3 (the decisions block), PI 4 (what G-PWR and v1.0 wait on; the Clock
  date).
- V1-misquote-9.3-then / A60 / B49: the §9.3 quotation is given without "then".
- V1-PI-order-attribution / A13: "in the PI's word's order and the read's ..., the table's place in it Code's reading
  (Q7)". Q7 quotes the PI's word and R2 3 in full.
- V1-Q9-cost: the table-order question (now Q7) states its cost and qualifies the step-14 read's Q4.
- V1-Q5-settled: the truncations move to a settled note. Q5 keeps only the closure, on lock §11 item 8's ground.
- V1-Q6-note: the decisions block moves to a note.
- V1-Q8-governing-words / B37 / B39 / B40: the stale-text question (now Q6) adds PIN-7's omission, R2 2's "On the texts"
  sentence, the allowance's scope (PIN-9 among the listed texts, PIN-10 not), lock §1 :558-:560, and "the last

======================================== THE SCRIPT
export const meta = {
  name: 'step15-ping-valuescheck',
  description: 'Values-check Code step-15 ping to the seat: two claim verifiers, strict refuter, values lens',
  phases: [
    { title: 'Verify', detail: 'every atomic claim, two halves' },
    { title: 'Refute', detail: 'strict refuter on flagged claims' },
    { title: 'Values', detail: 'values lens' },
  ],
}
const REPO = '/Users/jasondwyer/Documents/code/jspace-lab'
const SCR = '/private/tmp/claude-501/-Users-jasondwyer-Documents-code-jspace-lab/adb70afc-5886-490b-a675-abb30f8c425f/scratchpad/step15'
const PASS = args && args.pass ? args.pass : 1
const COMMON = `
CONTEXT. Repository ${REPO}. Code's step-15 ping to the design seat, for the PI to route, is drafted at ${SCR}/ping15.md (to be committed as results/raw/2026-10-14_m-code-to-design_step15.md beside the RESULTS entry "2026-10-14 — THE R2 3 ANALYZER ROUND AND THE G-ANL-F0 RE-RUN", uncommitted at the top of RESULTS.md). This is values-check pass ${PASS} before commit. Sources: the analyzer commit b046b16 (scripts/m_analyze.py, sha256 522e8057...fd3c; the review raw results/raw/2026-10-14_m-analyzer-round-step15_review.txt); the new manifest data/m/gates/G-ANL-F0.json and the moved one data/m/gates/G-ANL-F0.superseded-2026-10-14.json; LOCK-m-F0.md (v0.2 at top; the v0.1 text from "# The v0.1 text", its §§1, 9.1, 9.3, 9.6, 10, 11); PROPOSAL-m-gating-campaign-v1_11.md (m:N = line N); PREPRINT-v1_0-draft.md (the root copy; git ls-tree note-2026-10-v1); the seat's rulings results/raw/2026-10-10_m-design-to-code_pre-LOCK-v0.1.md (R1) and ..._round2.md (R2); the step-10 read results/raw/2026-10-13_m-design-to-code_G-TIME-F0_read.md; the step-14 read results/raw/2026-10-13_m-design-to-code_G-F0m-Qwen_read.md; RESULTS.md and git show 6920b4b:RESULTS.md; valence_core/m/bars.py, stages.py, schedule.py; scripts/m_sessions.py; DESIGN-to-CODE-PROPOSAL-m-v1.2-decisions.md (untracked); the review rounds under ${SCR} (r1_reviews.json ... r3_reviews.json, build/report_r3.md).
HARD RULES: read-only; no edits; no git command that writes; never run git inside ${REPO}/jlens-qwen36; no model; NEVER open data/m/ckpt/*, results/raw/2026-10-13_m-gf0m-qwen.txt or data/m/reports/Q-F0_qual_report.json; compute no contrast timing.`
const CLAIMS = { type: 'object', properties: { claims: { type: 'array', items: { type: 'object', properties: {
  id: { type: 'string' }, text: { type: 'string' }, verdict: { type: 'string', enum: ['VERIFIED', 'FAILS', 'IMPRECISE', 'UNCHECKABLE'] },
  evidence: { type: 'string' }, correction: { type: 'string' } }, required: ['id', 'text', 'verdict', 'evidence', 'correction'] } } }, required: ['claims'] }
const REF = { type: 'object', properties: { verdicts: { type: 'array', items: { type: 'object', properties: {
  id: { type: 'string' }, verdict: { type: 'string', enum: ['CONFIRMED', 'REFUTED', 'PLAUSIBLE'] }, evidence: { type: 'string' }, correction: { type: 'string' } },
  required: ['id', 'verdict', 'evidence', 'correction'] } } }, required: ['verdicts'] }
const VALUES = { type: 'object', properties: { findings: { type: 'array', items: { type: 'object', properties: {
  id: { type: 'string' }, severity: { type: 'string', enum: ['major', 'minor', 'note'] }, finding: { type: 'string' }, suggested_text: { type: 'string' } },
  required: ['id', 'severity', 'finding', 'suggested_text'] } } }, required: ['findings'] }
const VT = (half, p) => `${COMMON}\nTASK: extract EVERY atomic claim in ${half} of the ping (every quotation, line cite, hash, count, attribution, default, cost) and check each with tools. Quotations must be verbatim from their source (say where any differs). A paraphrase of the seat or the PI must not change meaning or scope. Number claims ${p}01, ... Verdicts VERIFIED / FAILS / IMPRECISE / UNCHECKABLE with evidence and exact minimal corrections.`
phase('Verify')
const vs = await parallel([
  ['the FIRST HALF (from the title through the end of Q3, the four PI items included)', 'A'],
  ['the SECOND HALF (from Q4 through the end, "Not questions" included)', 'B'],
].map(([h, p]) => () => agent(VT(h, p), { label: `verify-${p}`, phase: 'Verify', schema: CLAIMS })))
const claims = vs.filter(Boolean).flatMap(x => x.claims || [])
const flagged = claims.filter(c => c.verdict === 'FAILS' || c.verdict === 'IMPRECISE')
log(`pass ${PASS}: ${claims.length} claims, ${flagged.length} flagged`)
let r = null
if (flagged.length) {
  r = await agent(`${COMMON}\nTASK (STRICT refuter): for each flagged claim, re-check independently and try to show the ping's text is right. CONFIRMED = it must change (minimal exact correction); REFUTED = right as written; PLAUSIBLE = unsettled.\nFLAGGED:\n${JSON.stringify(flagged, null, 1)}`, { label: 'refute', phase: 'Refute', schema: REF })
}
phase('Values')
const vals = await agent(`${COMMON}\nTASK (values lens${PASS > 1 ? '; pass ' + PASS + ', after pass 1 was applied' : ''}): read the ping whole as the seat and the PI will. Is each question necessary, clearly put, with its governing words exact, Code's default and the cost of each answer stated, and nothing pre-decided that is the seat's or the PI's? Is any question missing that the step-15 record raises (PIN-17/§8, the preprint, m:58 re-anchoring, the decisions block, G-PWR methods, truncation, the new readings, the table's place, R3M-5, PIN-9/PIN-10)? Is anything presented as a question that the record already settles (then it should be a note), or as a note that needs the seat's answer? Is the item for the PI separated from the seat's? Any overclaim (e.g. about what the battery or the mutation batch shows), or attribution error? Blinding: nothing pick-bearing. Suggested replacement text for each finding.`, { label: 'values', phase: 'Values', schema: VALUES })
return { pass: PASS, claims, refuted: r ? r.verdicts : [], values: vals ? vals.findings : [] }

======================================== CODE'S DISPOSITIONS
Pass 2. The two verifiers checked 136 claims: 120 VERIFIED, 12 IMPRECISE, 1 FAILS, 3 UNCHECKABLE. Of the flags, 7 were
CONFIRMED and 6 REFUTED. The values lens returned 14 findings (3 major). Code rewrote the ping as v3 and applied every
confirmed flag and every values finding. The optional suggestions on refuted flags were applied too, at no cost: A08's
read list, A28's "as §4 item 3 asks", A44's provenance of the "" reading, A54's "F-1 and its refuter", A63's "point at the
root copy's §8.2", and B48's lowercase quotation.
- V2-PI1-window (major): the table waits for the PI's word on PI 1, in the routing that carries the ping; without one
  there, it is built on the default. This is mirrored in the entry.
- V2-PIN-index-missing (major): the PIN-index changes are in Q1, with Code's default for v1.0's addendum (verified by
  Code's comparison of the marker blocks).
- V2-Q3Q4-table (major): Q3 and Q4 now say what they bear on in the sentence table and when they are cheapest; so does
  the short form.
- V2-Q6-R2-2-scope / B24: the stale-texts question (now Q5) quotes R2 2's sentence in its scope and calls PIN-9's and
  PIN-10's clauses the reverse case. It states Code's reading of the misparaphrase allowance and a cost that holds now
  b046b16 has landed. PIN-10's own statement is included.
- V2-Q2-open-routes / A55: the second route (a relabel with the id moved off the record) is added. The check's safety
  now rests on stages.py:2306-2310. The candidates' guard is stated correctly.
- V2-Q1-c-widening: Code's ground for building (c) wider is given, and Q7 refers to Q1's terms.
- V2-Q5-to-note: the closure is a settled note, and the questions are renumbered: Q1 terms; Q2 R3M-5; Q3 PIN-17; Q4
  re-anchoring; Q5 stale texts; Q6 the table's place; Q7 G-PWR's methods.
- V2-PI4-Q4B / A15: (B) is the exception only if the seat adopts it. The cheapest-before lists are given, and so is D1's
  source.
- V2-Q8-guard-union: the guard's figure is the union of both halves, computed jointly. That is Code's default.
- V2-Q7-wording: Q6 states the letter of the listed order and proposes post-hoc acceptance as the seat's call.
- V2-PI1-deadline: §4 lists the placement inside step 15. How the deadlines combine is not Code's to settle.
- V2-PI3-cost, V2-PIN45-limits-ground, V2-Q4-precedent-and-reads: applied.
- A19, B09, B34, B46: applied.
- V2-blinding: no change.
- Added by Code: a "While the table waits" note (scratch-only preparation; G-PWR runs only after the v1.0 draft names
  the analyzer).

export const meta = {
  name: 'step15-entry-ping-pass3',
  description: 'Targeted third check of the step-15 entry and ping after pass 2 rewrites: verifier and values lens per document',

    { title: 'Verify', detail: 'claims in the changed parts, both documents' },

CONTEXT. Repository ${REPO}. const CLAIMS = { type: 'object', properties: { claims: { type: 'array', items: { type: 'object', properties: {
  id: { type: 'string' }, text: { type: 'string' }, verdict: { type: 'string', enum: ['VERIFIED', 'FAILS', 'IMPRECISE', 'UNCHECKABLE'] },
  evidence: { type: 'string' }, correction: { type: 'string' } }, required: ['id', 'text', 'verdict', 'evidence', 'correction'] } } }, required: ['claims'] }
const VALUES = { type: 'object', properties: { findings: { type: 'array', items: { type: 'object', properties: {
  id: { type: 'string' }, severity: { type: 'string', enum: ['major', 'minor', 'note'] }, finding: { type: 'string' }, suggested_text: { type: 'string' } },
  required: ['id', 'severity', 'finding', 'suggested_text'] } } }, required: ['findings'] }
phase('Verify')
const vs = await parallel([
  ['the ENTRY: every bullet the pass-2 dispositions name as changed (the Next list item 1; the sentence-table bullet; Code\'s readings list incl. (c); the PIN index bullet; Review\'s blinding line; the window bullet; "Corrections to b046b16\'s records"; the re-run stamps; D3; the site-check stamps; For the seat incl. PIN-45 limits, the closure item, "What v1.0\'s commit follows"; the preprint bullet; the stamp line)', 'E'],
  ['the PING v3: every claim in the short form, PI 1 to PI 4, Q1 (incl. the PIN-index bullet and the (c) ground), Q2 (incl. the stages.py:2306-2310 ground and the two open routes), Q5 (the R2 2 quotation in full), Q6, Q7(b) (the guard union) and the "Not questions" (the closure note, PIN-45 limits, While the table waits)', 'P'],
].map(([h, p]) => () => agent(`${COMMON}\nTASK: extract every atomic claim in ${h} and check each with tools; quotations verbatim; inclusive claims need an inventory. Number ${p}01... Verdicts VERIFIED / FAILS / IMPRECISE / UNCHECKABLE with evidence and exact minimal corrections.`, { label: `verify-${p}`, phase: 'Verify', schema: CLAIMS })))
const claims = vs.filter(Boolean).flatMap(x => x.claims || [])
phase('Values')
const vals = await agent(`${COMMON}\nTASK (values lens): read the entry and the ping side by side. (1) Do they agree everywhere (question numbers the entry cites, the table's waiting rule, the closure, PIN-45's limits, what v1.0 follows, the PIN index, (c)'s widening)? (2) Does either pre-decide anything that is the PI's (the copy; the deadlines; whether G-PWR/v1.0 wait) or the seat's (the readings' terms; the order's acceptance; Q4's reading)? (3) Is the "While the table waits" note acceptable under R1 A2 and the step-14 read's Q4 rule ("a build whose checks read a lock's committed text starts after that lock's commit, or states the departure in its review raw at the time"), or does preparing G-PWR's computation before the table depart from the listed order? (4) Anything pass 2's rewrite broke. Suggested replacement text for each finding.`, { label: 'values', phase: 'Values', schema: VALUES })
return { claims, values: vals ? vals.findings : [] }

======================================== CODE'S DISPOSITIONS
Pass 3, a targeted check of both documents after pass 2's rewrites. One verifier per document checked 166 claims: 149
VERIFIED, 11 IMPRECISE, 5 FAILS, 1 UNCHECKABLE. A values lens read the two documents side by side and returned 12
findings, 3 of them major. This check had no strict refuter. Code checked each flag itself before applying it, and
applied every flag and every finding.

Major:
- VC3-01: "While the table waits" is rewritten. G-PWR runs only in the listed order: after the table, after the v1.0
  draft names the analyzer and the table, and after the seat's answers if the PI holds it for them. Its raw records the
  draft's sha256.
  - Scratch preparation of G-PWR's computation, tested on synthetic inputs only, is Code's reading, put to the seat with
    Q6. The v1.0 draft is not prepared ahead.
- VC3-02 / E63: the entry's "What v1.0's commit follows" now carries Q4(B) as the exception, and says that holding the
  table, G-PWR or v1.0 is the PI's call.
- VC3-03 / P89 / P54 / P55: PIN-45's second and third limits are stated as PIN-45 states them, in both documents. Ids
  cannot tell the relabel apart only at the arm's next-launch id; elsewhere it passes because V2 checks the id against
  the record alone.

Minor:
- VC3-04 / E55: PI 4 now includes holding the table, and gives a default without a word. The short form says when an
  answer to Q3 or Q4 rebuilds the table.
- VC3-05: the table waits for the PI's word to Code. That word may name a copy, or name none, which means the root copy.
  The table no longer waits on "silence in the routing", which Code cannot observe. Both documents are changed.
- VC3-06: the entry's stale-texts cost and its item.
- VC3-07: Q2's default states the remaining wider scope.
- VC3-08: the entry gives the listed order's letter, and Q6 quotes the PI's word in full with the read's §4 item 4. The
  parallel between (c)'s widening and the table's place is now exact.
- VC3-09 / E62 / P84: the closure note shows that the step-10 read's Q1 conditions only the package, and that PIN-14 is
  the only check that reads the closure. §9.1 already truncates it.
- VC3-10: the Clock date is stated as m:324's NOT-RUN path, open to the PI. Whether the root copy meets m:356 is the PI's
  to say.
- VC3-11 / E45: two more corrections to b046b16's records: the 33 are 23 as written and 10 re-expressed (Code recounted
  them from mutants_extra_r6.json); and "needed" is Code's reading.
- VC3-12 / E67: the values-check raw's description, the pass-3 stamp, and the ping's read list (step-14 read Q3 and Q4).

Flags from the claim check:
- E12: the m:356 sentence matches the close line.
- E64: D3's waits-on line.
- P09: Q6's cost of an answer.
- P43: Code's round-4 dispositions as a source of readings.
- P85: the driver self-test's case also reads the analyzer's sha256, which v1.0 names in full.
- P95: L2-9's full list.
- P98: the table-waits note's reading is labelled.

After pass 3, a mechanical quote check over both documents ran on every double-quoted span of 12 or more characters
(output below). 8 of 119 spans did not match the script's source set. Code confirmed each by hand:
- R2 3 with its ellipses, and m:330 with its ellipsis: checked by the passes;

- the entry's own heading;
- PIN-17's register text: its PINS value starts with the quoted text;
- R2 item 2's sentence, quoted with its inner double quotes as single quotes.
